Capacity proof: C47660 calls C4A070 with ECX=compound+4 and arguments (4,1) at C47727. C4A070 stores requested capacity at array+C, hence compound+10. Array vtable allocator +4=C4A860 allocates 100*requested entries. Constructor starts with compound+24=0, +28=0 and frustum capacity 4, not 1024. Larger capacity is supported by C4A070; actual Fairfax capacity is unknown. A first local snapshot admission could match exactly the native four-slot capacity, requiring 16 mask bytes, retaining native allocation for other capacities; structure stability and complete callback/provider qualification remain open.

Current replacement cost, direct source evidence: Runtime::alloc and Runtime::free in psycho-engine-fixes/src/mods/heap_replacer/scrap_heap/runtime.rs use an identity/generation TLC cache on hits. Successful ordinary alloc calls Heap::try_alloc_slow_with_provider; valid free calls Heap::free, both in scrap_heap/heap.rs. Each takes self.state.lock(). Therefore a successful native compound snapshot/free pair requests two per-identity heap-state mutex acquisitions plus header/count/rewind work. The cold map path is conditional. Contention, backing acquisition, GC and Fairfax invocation counts are not established by this source evidence. No allocator implementation change is proposed.
